> $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
> 
> -- (trigger tr_encrypt_user_api_keys already bound to the function — no re-create needed)
> 
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> -- 3. MASKED PUBLIC VIEW — append apify_token (masked)
> -- MUST match the CURRENT production definition from 20260601000600_secure_definer_views.sql:
> -- a SECURITY DEFINER view (default, NOT security_invoker — base-table SELECT is REVOKED from
> -- authenticated) with an explicit WHERE tenancy filter. Reverting to security_invoker here would
> -- break authenticated reads (permission denied on the base table).
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.user_api_keys AS
> SELECT
>   user_id,
>   created_at,
>   updated_at,
>   pref_audio_provider,
>   pref_image_provider,
>   pref_video_provider,
>   wp_site_url,
>   wp_username,
>   pref_ai_model,
>   CASE WHEN groq_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS groq_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN openrouter_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS openrouter_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN openai_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS openai_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN elevenlabs_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS elevenlabs_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN google_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS google_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN nanobana_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS nanobana_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN seedance_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS seedance_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN wp_app_password IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS wp_app_password,
>   CASE WHEN replicate_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS replicate_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN higgsfield_api_key IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS higgsfield_api_key,
>   CASE WHEN higgsfield_api_key_secret IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS higgsfield_api_key_secret,
>   CASE WHEN apify_token IS NOT NULL THEN '••••••••••••' ELSE NULL END AS apify_token
> FROM public.user_api_keys_table
> WHERE (auth.uid() = user_id OR auth.role() = 'service_role');
> 
> -- Preserve client grants (CREATE OR REPLACE keeps ACLs; re-assert to be safe / idempotent).
> GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON public.user_api_keys TO anon, authenticated;
> 
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> -- 4. INSTEAD OF TRIGGER — add apify_token to INSERT cols/VALUES + ON CONFLICT UPDATE
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.trg_instead_of_user_api_keys()
> RETURNS TRIGGER AS $$
> BEGIN
>   IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
>     DELETE FROM public.user_api_keys_table WHERE user_id = OLD.user_id;
>     RETURN OLD;
>   ELSIF TG_OP = 'INSERT' OR TG_OP = 'UPDATE' THEN
>     INSERT INTO public.user_api_keys_table (
>       user_id, groq_api_key, openrouter_api_key, openai_api_key, pref_audio_provider, pref_image_provider, pref_video_provider, elevenlabs_api_key, google_api_key, nanobana_api_key, seedance_api_key, wp_site_url, wp_username, wp_app_password, pref_ai_model, replicate_api_key, higgsfield_api_key, higgsfield_api_key_secret, apify_token
>     ) VALUES (
>       NEW.user_id, NEW.groq_api_key, NEW.openrouter_api_key, NEW.openai_api_key, NEW.pref_audio_provider, NEW.pref_image_provider, NEW.pref_video_provider, NEW.elevenlabs_api_key, NEW.google_api_key, NEW.nanobana_api_key, NEW.seedance_api_key, NEW.wp_site_url, NEW.wp_username, NEW.wp_app_password, NEW.pref_ai_model, NEW.replicate_api_key, NEW.higgsfield_api_key, NEW.higgsfield_api_key_secret, NEW.apify_token
>     )
>     ON CONFLICT (user_id) DO UPDATE SET
>       groq_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.groq_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.groq_api_key),
>       openrouter_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.openrouter_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.openrouter_api_key),
>       openai_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.openai_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.openai_api_key),
>       pref_audio_provider = COALESCE(NEW.pref_audio_provider, public.user_api_keys_table.pref_audio_provider),
>       pref_image_provider = COALESCE(NEW.pref_image_provider, public.user_api_keys_table.pref_image_provider),
>       pref_video_provider = COALESCE(NEW.pref_video_provider, public.user_api_keys_table.pref_video_provider),
>       elevenlabs_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.elevenlabs_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.elevenlabs_api_key),
>       google_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.google_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.google_api_key),
>       nanobana_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.nanobana_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.nanobana_api_key),
>       seedance_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.seedance_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.seedance_api_key),
>       wp_site_url = NEW.wp_site_url,
>       wp_username = NEW.wp_username,
>       wp_app_password = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.wp_app_password, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.wp_app_password),
>       pref_ai_model = COALESCE(NEW.pref_ai_model, public.user_api_keys_table.pref_ai_model),
>       replicate_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.replicate_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.replicate_api_key),
>       higgsfield_api_key = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.higgsfield_api_key, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.higgsfield_api_key),
>       higgsfield_api_key_secret = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.higgsfield_api_key_secret, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.higgsfield_api_key_secret),
>       apify_token = COALESCE(NULLIF(NEW.apify_token, '••••••••••••'), public.user_api_keys_table.apify_token),
>       updated_at = now();
>     RETURN NEW;
>   END IF;
>   RETURN NULL;
> END;
> $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER;
> 
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> -- 5. DECRYPTED VIEW (service_role only) — append apify_token + its vault join
> -- ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> CREATE OR REPLACE VIEW public.decrypted_user_api_keys AS
> SELECT
>   uak.user_id,
>   COALESCE(s_groq.decrypted_secret, uak.groq_api_key) AS groq_api_key,
>   COALESCE(s_openrouter.decrypted_secret, uak.openrouter_api_key) AS openrouter_api_key,
>   COALESCE(s_openai.decrypted_secret, uak.openai_api_key) AS openai_api_key,
>   uak.created_at,
>   uak.updated_at,
>   uak.pref_audio_provider,
>   uak.pref_image_provider,
>   uak.pref_video_provider,
>   COALESCE(s_elevenlabs.decrypted_secret, uak.elevenlabs_api_key) AS elevenlabs_api_key,
>   COALESCE(s_google.decrypted_secret, uak.google_api_key) AS google_api_key,
>   COALESCE(s_nanobana.decrypted_secret, uak.nanobana_api_key) AS nanobana_api_key,
>   COALESCE(s_seedance.decrypted_secret, uak.seedance_api_key) AS seedance_api_key,
>   uak.wp_site_url,
>   uak.wp_username,
>   COALESCE(s_wp_pwd.decrypted_secret, uak.wp_app_password) AS wp_app_password,
>   uak.pref_ai_model,
>   COALESCE(s_replicate.decrypted_secret, uak.replicate_api_key) AS replicate_api_key,
>   COALESCE(s_higgsfield.decrypted_secret, uak.higgsfield_api_key) AS higgsfield_api_key,
>   COALESCE(s_higgsfield_sec.decrypted_secret, uak.higgsfield_api_key_secret) AS higgsfield_api_key_secret,
>   COALESCE(s_apify.decrypted_secret, uak.apify_token) AS apify_token
> FROM public.user_api_keys_table uak
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_groq ON uak.groq_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_groq.id = uak.groq_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_openrouter ON uak.openrouter_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_openrouter.id = uak.openrouter_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_openai ON uak.openai_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_openai.id = uak.openai_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_elevenlabs ON uak.elevenlabs_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_elevenlabs.id = uak.elevenlabs_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_google ON uak.google_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_google.id = uak.google_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_nanobana ON uak.nanobana_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_nanobana.id = uak.nanobana_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_seedance ON uak.seedance_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_seedance.id = uak.seedance_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_wp_pwd ON uak.wp_app_password ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_wp_pwd.id = uak.wp_app_password::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_replicate ON uak.replicate_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_replicate.id = uak.replicate_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_higgsfield ON uak.higgsfield_api_key ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_higgsfield.id = uak.higgsfield_api_key::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_higgsfield_sec ON uak.higgsfield_api_key_secret ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_higgsfield_sec.id = uak.higgsfield_api_key_secret::uuid
> LEFT JOIN vault.decrypted_secrets s_apify ON uak.apify_token ~ '^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$' AND s_apify.id = uak.apify_token::uuid;
> 
> -- decrypted_user_api_keys stays service_role-only (grants preserved across REPLACE; re-assert to be safe)
> REVOKE ALL ON public.decrypted_user_api_keys FROM PUBLIC, anon, authenticated;
> GRANT SELECT ON public.decrypted_user_api_keys TO service_role;
> ====COMPOSE====
> # docker-compose.yml — MCORCH Sovereign Mesh (hardened — docs/bok/security/03-sdd-hardening-architecture.md §5)
> # FM-04 fix: the revoked SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY env is removed (SB_SECRET_KEY is the only live credential).
> # FM-10 fix: mcorch_gitnexus runs as UID 1001 (hardened Dockerfile §4), read-only rootfs, dropped capabilities.
> # NOTE: no `name:` field — the project must stay the directory default (`constellation-orchestra`) so a
> # `docker compose up -d` RECREATES the existing running containers instead of orphaning them under a new
> # project (container_name collision). Verified 2026-06-10: running containers are project=constellation-orchestra.
> 
> x-hardening: &hardening
>   restart: unless-stopped
>   security_opt:
>     - no-new-privileges:true        # setuid binaries cannot escalate (defense in depth for dropped caps)
>   cap_drop:
>     - CHOWN                          # blueprint-mandated drop set:
>     - DAC_OVERRIDE                   #   file-permission bypass
>     - SETUID                         #   privilege transitions
>     - SETGID                         #   group transitions
>     - NET_RAW                        # + raw sockets (ARP/ICMP spoofing inside the bridge)
>     - MKNOD                          # + device node creation
>     - FOWNER                         # + ownership-check bypass
>     - AUDIT_WRITE                    # + kernel audit-log tampering
>   logging:
>     driver: json-file
>     options:
>       max-size: "10m"               # a compromised container cannot disk-flood via stdout
>       max-file: "3"
> 
> services:
>   mcorch-intelligence-node:
>     <<: *hardening
>     image: infra-autonomic-daemon:latest   # local ARM64 build; thedotmack/claude-mem unavailable
> ====GITNEXUS====
>     # The dropped capabilities and resource limits above still apply.
>     networks:
>       - mcorch-sovereign-net
> 
>   mcorch-gitnexus-mcp:
>     <<: *hardening
>     build:
>       context: ./packages/gitnexus-core
>       dockerfile: Dockerfile          # hardened multi-stage image (SDD §4, USER 1001, dev-tools stripped)
>     container_name: mcorch_gitnexus
>     user: "1001:1001"
>     read_only: true                   # server is stateless; AST index lives in memory
>     tmpfs:
>       - /tmp:size=64m
>     ports:
>       - "127.0.0.1:3100:3100"        # HTTP MCP transport — Claude Code + n8n
>     cpus: "1.00"
>     mem_limit: 768m
>     pids_limit: 256
>     environment:
>       - NODE_ENV=production
>       - TARGET_DIR=/workspace/src     # scan only src/ — skip node_modules
>       - PORT=3100
>       - MAX_FILE_SIZE_BYTES=1000000
>     volumes:
>       - ./:/workspace:ro              # mount entire project read-only
>     healthcheck:
>       # wget was stripped from the hardened image — node's built-in fetch replaces it.
>       test: ["CMD", "node", "-e", "fetch('http://127.0.0.1:3100/health').then(r=>process.exit(r.ok?0:1)).catch(()=>process.exit(1))"]
>       interval: 30s
>       timeout: 5s
>       retries: 3
>       start_period: 60s               # allow time for initial AST ingest
>     networks:
>       - mcorch-sovereign-net
> 
> networks:
>   mcorch-sovereign-net:
> ```

do layer map:** componentes display-only \u2192 hooks (toda l\u00f3gica de dados) \u2192 tabelas RLS. O plano de dados quente (tool calls, billing, mesh) NUNCA passa pelo frontend \u2014 vive inteiro no container, que fala com o Supabase por PostgREST/RPC com o secret de nome `SB_SECRET_KEY` (server-side only, jamais em path client-reachable \u2014 NFR-VM-007).\n\n### 1.2 Runtime Decision \u2014 Container no Sovereign Mesh (selado)\n\n**Decis\u00e3o:** servi\u00e7o long-lived `mcorch_vision_mcp` no Docker Sovereign Mesh, rede `mcorch-sovereign-net`, publicado loopback-only (`127.0.0.1:3200`) atr\u00e1s do nginx vhost `mcp.mcorch.com`. Pacote em `packages/vision-mcp-core/` (precedente `packages/gitnexus-core/`).\n\nFundamenta\u00e7\u00e3o (blueprint \u00a75.1, confirmada materialmente):\n\n1. **Precedente direto.** `mcorch_gitnexus` j\u00e1 roda MCP server in-house com `StreamableHTTPServerTransport` em `127.0.0.1:3100` (`packages/gitnexus-core/src/mcp/server.ts:16,97`; `docker-compose.yml:89-119`). O envelope de hardening \u00e9 reutiliz\u00e1vel as-is: anchor `x-hardening` (`docker-compose.yml:8` \u2014 `no-new-privileges`, `cap_drop` de 8 capabilities, log rotation), `user: \"1001:1001\"`, `read_only: true` + tmpfs, `cpus`/`mem_limit`/`pids_limit`, healthcheck via `node -e fetch(...)` (wget removido da imagem hardened).\n2. **Timeouts matam a rota Edge.** O hist\u00f3rico do repo prova que wall-clock de Edge Function for\u00e7ou o orchestrate a virar passos async via pg_net (`docs/processes/orchestrate-async-pipeline.md`). Deepsearch \u00e9 trabalho de minutos \u2192 **zero novas Edge Functions** (FRD \u00a71 out-of-scope); jobs ass\u00edncronos vivem dentro do container (FR-VM-007). Reuso apenas: `embed-mcorch-node`, `get-infra-status`, RPC `deduct_mco_coins`.\n3. **Alternativa systemd REJEITADA.** Rodar bare no host (padr\u00e3o openclaw gateway) regrediria o BoK de seguran\u00e7a: o FM-10 da su\u00edte Cyber-Sentinel foi corrigido justamente movendo o gitnexus para container hardened n\u00e3o-root. Um servi\u00e7o tenant-facing/billable exige o mesmo envelope `cap_drop`/`no-new-privileges`/`mem_limit`.\n\n**Caveats materiais herdados:** N\u00c3O adicionar campo `name:` ao `docker-compose.yml` (header do compose: o project deve permanecer `constellation-orchestra` ou os containers viram \u00f3rf\u00e3os). Single-replica por design at\u00e9 rate limiter compartilhado (NFR-VM-004, OTD-VM-014).\n\n### 1.3 Two-Tier \"Ess\u00eancia Humana\" Pipeline\n\n```\n input (imagem/v\u00eddeo/screen/URL)\n \u25bc\n\u250c\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2510\n\u2502 KNOWLEDGE MESH \u2014 mcorch_nodes/edges (user_id + project_id)    \u2502\n\u2502 PII-strip \u2192 n\u00f3 observation/fact + provenance/license/         \u2502\n\u2502 retention_until \u2192 embed-mcorch-node (pgvector 768, gr\u00e1tis)    \u2502\n\u2502 NUNCA system nodes (user_id IS NULL) p/ conte\u00fado scraped      \u2502\n\u2514\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2518\n```\n\n**Economia (active inference \u2192 metering):** o Tier 1 roda em CPU quase-gr\u00e1tis e s\u00f3 dispara a f\u00f3vea cara quando o ganho de informa\u00e7\u00e3o esperado justifica o custo \u2014 mapeado literalmente no metering mcoCoins (a f\u00f3vea debita, a periferia quase n\u00e3o). **Honestidade de limite (hip\u00f3teses marcadas, n\u00e3o achados):** eventos sint\u00e9ticos n\u00e3o entregam resolu\u00e7\u00e3o temporal \u00b5s (timestamps interpolados \u2014 blueprint Pilar III); transfer\u00eancia de sali\u00eancia DeepGaze para telas/UIs \u00e9 n\u00e3o-comprovada (OTD-VM-001); o argumento sim-to-real \"se dissolve em dom\u00ednio de tela\" \u00e9 racioc\u00ednio, n\u00e3o evid\u00eancia (OTD-VM-012). Por isso o gate perif\u00e9rico nasce `experimental` com condi\u00e7\u00e3o de GA = recall medido \u22650.95 provis\u00f3rio (FR-VM-010).\n\n### 1.4 Cognitive Loop (CoALA) \u2014 percep\u00e7\u00e3o como \u00f3rg\u00e3o\n\n\u00c2ncoras cit\u00e1veis: CoALA (TMLR 2024), V*/SEAL (CVPR 2024), Microsoft GraphRAG. **Proibido** ancorar em \"cognitive computing\"/\"c\u00e9rebro digital\"/consci\u00eancia GWT (BR-VM-009, FM-VM-15).\n\n| CoALA | Realiza\u00e7\u00e3o MCORCH (existente \u2014 zero invento) |\n|---|---|\n| Percep\u00e7\u00e3o | Tools `vision.*`/`motion.*` deste servi\u00e7o |\n| Aten\u00e7\u00e3o | Tier 1 gate perif\u00e9rico (busca visual guiada V*/SEAL: passe amplo barato \u2192 pol\u00edtica \u2192 crop caro) |\n| Working memory | Janela de contexto do agente host |\n| Mem\u00f3ria epis\u00f3dica | N\u00f3s `observation` em `mcorch_nodes` \u2014 **decaem** (FSRS-6/`stability_score`) |\n| Mem\u00f3ria sem\u00e2ntica | Grafo `mcorch_edges` + pgvector(768) \u2014 **fatos suplantam** (supersession, sem decay) |\n| Mem\u00f3ria procedural | Skills/SOPs (`.claude/skills/`, `docs/processes/`) |\n| Broadcast (GWT \u2014 s\u00f3 met\u00e1fora) | Resultado publicado na malha, leg\u00edvel por todo o Pantheon |\n\n**Regime de persist\u00eancia (FR-VM-015, anti-category-error arXiv 2604.11364):** `mesh.consolidate_reference` exige `regime \u2208 {observation_decay, fact_supersede}` no insert; n\u00f3s `fact_supersede` criam edge `SUPERSEDES` para a vers\u00e3o anterior e ficam isentos do decay FSRS (design em \u00a73.5/\u00a77).\n\n---\n\n## 2. fail-closed; 401 + `WWW-Authenticate resource_metadata` (RFC 9728) | FR-VM-002 |\n| PAT | `auth/pat.ts` | Lookup SHA-256 em `mcp_access_tokens` (O(1) por hash), checa `expires_at`/`revoked_at`/scopes, atualiza `last_used_at` | FR-VM-003 |\n| Credentials | `auth/credentials.ts` | Resolu\u00e7\u00e3o per-user fail-closed via `decrypted_user_api_keys` (firecrawl/openrouter/google/mistral/openai); constantes `VISION_COIN_COSTS` hardcoded (espelhamento triplo c/ `src/lib/billing.ts` + `src/test/billing.test.ts`) | FR-VM-006 |\n| Tool registry | `tools/registry.ts` | Registro declarativo tool\u2192{classe mco, scope, gates, handler}; \u00fanica fonte de verdade da superf\u00edcie | FR-VM-006 |\n| Tier 1 | `tools/peripheral/` | `motion.peripheral_scan` \u2014 frame-diff/MOG2/event-synthesis/sali\u00eancia (CPU, ARM64) | FR-VM-010 |\n| Tier 2 vision | `tools/foveal/` | `vision.describe_image`/`detect_objects`/`segment`/`ocr`/`analyze_video` | FR-VM-018/019/024 |\n| Motion | `tools/motion/` | `motion.track` (RTMPose/MediaPipe + ByteTrack/BoT-SORT; keep-warm contra FM-VM-12) | FR-VM-024 |\n| Deepsearch | `tools/deepsearch/` | `deepsearch.scrape` (robots/login-wall/allowlist) + `deepsearch.run`/`poll` (m\u00e1quina de estados em `vision_jobs`) | FR-VM-020, FR-VM-007 |\n| Assistive | `tools/assistive/` | `vision.alt_text`/`describe_screen` (dual-backend BYOK, padr\u00e3o JAWS/NVDA) | FR-VM-012/013 |\n| Mesh writer | `mesh/consolidate.ts` | `mesh.search` (RPC `match_mcorch_nodes`) + `mesh.consolidate_reference` (PII-strip \u2192 n\u00f3 + provenance + regime + embed-on-insert + edge ao run) | FR-VM-017/021/008/015 |\n| Jobs engine | `jobs/engine.ts` | Fila in-process single-replica + persist\u00eancia `vision_jobs`; partial-delivery refund \u00a74.5 do FRD (idempotente por `job_id`) | FR-VM-007 |\n| Telemetry | `infra/telemetry.ts` | `infra_health_logs` fire-and-forget (`service='vision-mcp'`/`'sentinel'`) em todo path success/degraded/error/block/refusal | FR-VM-008, NFR-VM-010 |\n| Retention sweep | `jobs/retention-sweep.ts` | Job di\u00e1rio in-container: expira artefatos com `retention_until < now()` via `erase_vision_artifacts` | FR-VM-009 |\n\n### 2.2 Tier-2 Provider & Model Matrix (license lanes \u2014 FM-VM-08)\n\n**Pista Apache/permissiva \u00e9 o default.** Modelos gated/copyleft s\u00f3 entram com propaga\u00e7\u00e3o de licen\u00e7a documentada (LICENSE fetchado \u2014 OTD-VM-006) e nunca como \u00fanico caminho.\n\n| Capability | Default (lane permissiva) | Alternativa (condicionada) | Consumo |\n|---|---|---|---|\n| VLM describe/QA | Qwen3-VL (Apache-2.0, via OpenRouter BYOK) | Gemini/Claude/GPT-5.x (propriet\u00e1rias, BYOK per-user) | API |\n| Detection (boxes) | Gemini native boxes `[ymin,xmin,ymax,xmax]` (\u00fanico VLM-API com detection treinado-in) | RF-DETR core (Apache-2.0, self-host futuro) | API / GPU |\n| Segmentation | SAM 2 (Apache-2.0, Replicate ~$0.019/run) | SAM 3 (SAM License bespoke; fal $0.005/req) \u2014 s\u00f3 com OTD-VM-006 resolvida | hosted |\n| OCR | PaddleOCR 3.x (Apache-2.0, CPU in-container) | Mistral OCR 3 (propriet\u00e1ria, integra\u00e7\u00e3o direta per-user \u2014 N\u00c3O est\u00e1 no OpenRouter) | CPU / API |\n| Video QA | Gemini video API (1 FPS, ~300 tok/s) | TwelveLabs via Bedrock | API |\n| Optical flow / pose / track | SEA-RAFT (BSD-3) \u00b7 RTMPose/MediaPipe (Apache-2.0) \u00b7 ByteTrack/BoT-SORT (MIT) | \u2014 | CPU/GPU |\n| Event synthesis (Tier 1) | OpenEB-style (Apache-2.0) / frame-diff log-intensity pr\u00f3prio | v2e (MIT); rpg_vid2e EXCLU\u00cdDO (GPL-3.0) | CPU |\n| **PROIBIDO sem licen\u00e7a Enterprise** | \u2014 | Ultralytics YOLO (AGPL-3.0 \u2014 a maior armadilha do ecossistema p/ SaaS closed-source) | \u2014 |\n| Sali\u00eancia | DeepGaze IIE/III (research license \u2014 uso experimental only at\u00e9 OTD-VM-001) | pop-out FIT pr\u00f3prio (feature \u00fanica, paralelo) | CPU/GPU |\n\n**Pin selado:** OpenCV **4.x** (Apache-2.0 verificada); upgrade a 5.0 s\u00f3 ap\u00f3s re-verifica\u00e7\u00e3o de licen\u00e7a + bench ARM64 (resolve a recomenda\u00e7\u00e3o de OTD-VM-010; benchmarks aarch64 continuam pendentes na OTD).\n\n**Provider matrix assistivo (FR-VM-012/013):** dual-backend m\u00ednimo OpenAI + Anthropic (precedente JAWS PictureSmart) com fallback provider-agn\u00f3stico (precedente NVDA Content Describer) \u2014 tudo resolvido pelo BYOK per-user; sem provider default at\u00e9 auditoria de DPA/SCC (OTD-VM-005).\n\n### 2.3 New Custom Hooks (`src/hooks/`)\n\n| Hook | File | Responsibility | TanStack Query keys |\n|------|------|---------------|---------------------|\n| `useMcpTokens` | `useMcpTokens.ts` | Gera/lista/revoga PATs (gera\u00e7\u00e3o client-side: random 32B \u2192 SHA-256 \u2192 insert RLS; plaintext exibido UMA vez) | `['mcp-tokens', userId]` |\n| `useVisionMcp` | `useVisionMcp.ts` | Estado do connector (URL `mcp.mcorch.com`, health via `get-infra-status`, metadata do Plugin Zip versionado) | `['vision-connector', userId]` |\n| `useVisionUsage` | `useVisionUsage.ts` | Consumo mcoCoins por tool/tenant sobre `agent_executions` + ledger; margem por tool (recalibra\u00e7\u00e3o KPI) | `['vision-usage', userId]` |\n| `useUserApiKeys` (extens\u00e3o) | `useUserApiKeys.ts` | + `firecrawl_api_key`, `mistral_api_key` \u2014 write `.insert` (NUNCA `.upsert` \u2014 VIEW Vault), null n\u00e3o limpa (clear via RPC), teste server-side | `['user-api-keys', userId]` (existente) |\n\n### 2.4 New Display Components (`src/components/settings/`)\n\n| Component | File | Props | Hook consumed |\n|-----------|------|-------|--------------|\n| `VisionDeepsearchCard` | `settings/VisionDeepsearchCard.tsx` | `{ }` | `useUserApiKeys` |\n| `McpConnectorsCard` | `settings/McpConnectorsCard.tsx` | `{ }` | `useMcpTokens` + `useVisionMcp` |\n| `VisionUsagePanel` | `settings/VisionUsagePanel.tsx` | `{ }` | `useVisionUsage` |\n\nEstados obrigat\u00f3rios (FR-VM-022): conectado / n\u00e3o-configurado + CTA / erro com mensagem PT-BR acion\u00e1vel. WCAG 2.2 AA exemplar (NFR-VM-012 \u2014 o m\u00f3dulo VENDE acessibilidade).\n\n### 2.5 Edge Functions & Stores \u2014 decis\u00f5es negativas (seladas)\n\n- **Zero novas Edge Functions** (FRD \u00a71): nenhum shim Deno. Se algum shim vier a existir no futuro (ex.: webhook), segue a conven\u00e7\u00e3o do `supabase/config.toml` \u2014 `verify_jwt=false` + ES256 in-code (SOP `docs/processes/edge-jwt-identity-verification.md`); registrado aqui s\u00f3 como conven\u00e7\u00e3o, n\u00e3o como componente deste ciclo. Reuso: `embed-mcorch-node` (embed-on-insert), `get-infra-status` (HUD), RPC `deduct_mco_coins`.\n- **SEM novo Zustand store** \u2014 justificativa: n\u00e3o h\u00e1 estado de run client-side (jobs vivem no container e s\u00e3o consultados por poll), n\u00e3o h\u00e1 canvas/grafo interativo; todo dado remoto \u00e9 server state \u2192 TanStack Query (regra da casa). Um store s\u00f3 se justificaria com orquestra\u00e7\u00e3o visual de pipeline, fora deste ciclo.\n- **PAT sem endpoint REST de gest\u00e3o:** gera\u00e7\u00e3o/revoga\u00e7\u00e3o \u00e9 write RLS direto na tabela pelo hook (hash client-side; plaintext nunca trafega para o servidor) \u2014 elimina superf\u00edcie de ataque e mant\u00e9m o mandato zero-novas-edge-fns.\n\n### 2.6 New DB Objects\n\n| Object | Type | Purpose | Migration file |\n|--------|------|---------|---------------|\n| `mcp_access_tokens` | Table | PATs para clients MCP externos (hash SHA-256, scopes, expiry, revoke) | `<ts>_vision_mcp_access_tokens.sql` |\n| `vision_jobs` | Table | M\u00e1quina de estados de jobs async (`queued\u2192running\u2192done\\|partial\\|failed`), reten\u00e7\u00e3o poll \u226524h, refund idempotente | `<ts>_vision_mcp_jobs.sql` |\n| `vision_artifacts` | Table | Manifest de artefatos (frames/crops Storage, node_ids, c\u00f3pias provider, `retention_until`) \u2014 habilita o cascade de erasure | `<ts>_vision_mcp_artifacts.sql` |\n| `user_api_keys.firecrawl_api_key` / `.mistral_api_key` | Columns (VIEW family) | BYOK deepsearch + OCR direto (padr\u00e3o Vault da `20260601060000`) | `<ts>_user_api_keys_vision_byok.sql` |\n| `erase_vision_artifacts(content_ref)` | RPC (SECURITY DEFINER) | Cascade LGPD: n\u00f3+edges+embedding+Storage+registro de c\u00f3pias provider (espelha `erase_lead()`) | `<ts>_vision_mcp_erase_rpc.sql` |\n| `vision-artifacts` | Storage bucket (private) | Frames/crops servidos por signed URL curto (resource `vision://artifacts/{content_ref}`) | config + migration |\n\nToda migration passa `/security-review` antes do commit (FMEA-011 house rule; NFR-VM-007).\n\n---\n\n## 3. \u2502\u2500\u2500scrape\u00d7N (robots/allowlist/login-wall)\u2500\u25b6\u2502 Firecrawl BYOK\n \u2502                       \u2502\u2500\u2500sentinel L2 por p\u00e1gina \u2192 n\u00f3s na malha\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502  [SSE heartbeat \u226425s] \u2502\u2500\u2500estado terminal:                        \u2502\n \u2502                       \u2502   delivered=planned \u2192 done               \u2502\n \u2502                       \u2502   0<delivered<planned \u2192 partial:         \u2502\n \u2502                       \u2502     retida=max(1,floor(3\u00d7del/planned))   \u2502\n \u2502                       \u2502     refundMco(3\u2212retida) UMA vez (guard   \u2502\n \u2502                       \u2502     por job_id \u2014 idempotente)            \u2502\n \u2502                       \u2502   delivered=0 \u2192 failed + refund integral \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500deepsearch.poll\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 mesma identidade (outro tenant \u2192 404)    \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500{state,delivered_units,partial,failed_units[],refunded_mco,result}\n \u2502   (resultado poll-able \u226524h p\u00f3s-terminal \u2014 vision_jobs.expires_at)\n```\n\n### 3.4 Tier 1 \u2192 Tier 2 trigger (Ess\u00eancia Humana \u2014 FR-VM-010)\n\n```\nClient/agent            Tier 1 (CPU)                       Tier 2 (billable)\n \u2502\u2500\u2500motion.peripheral_scan\u25b6 frame-diff/MOG2 + event-synth     \u2502\n \u2502   (0\u20131 mco)            \u2502 + sali\u00eancia \u2192 regi\u00f5es salientes   \u2502\n \u2502\u25c0\u2500{regions[], resource_link vision://artifacts/{ref}}       \u2502\n \u2502   (crops em Storage privado, signed URL curto)             \u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500 decis\u00e3o do agente (active-inference: vale o custo?) \u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502\n \u2502\u2500\u2500vision.describe_image{image_url: <crop signed URL>}\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u2500\u25b6\u2502 fluxo \u00a73.1 (2 mco)\n \u2502   [bypass: client pode chamar Tier 2 direto SEMPRE \u2014       \u2502\n \u2502    gate experimental n\u00e3o \u00e9 gargalo obrigat\u00f3rio]            \u2502\n```\n\n### 3.5 Mesh consolidation (`mesh.consolidate_reference` \u2014 FR-VM-021/015)\n\n```\nTool output (vision+deepsearch)\n  \u2192 PII-strip (server-side, pr\u00e9-insert \u2014 FM-VM-10)\n  \u2192 sentinel L2 sobre conte\u00fado (FM-VM-03 \u2014 inje\u00e7\u00e3o persistente nunca vira n\u00f3)\n  \u2192 INSERT mcorch_nodes { node_type:'observation'|'fact', user_id (NUNCA NULL p/ scraped),\n      project_id, metadata: { regime, provenance:{source_url,license,origin}, retention_until,\n      cost_mco, fr_id:'FR-VM-021', origin_tag:'scraped'|'tenant' }, stability_score: 1.0 }\n  \u2192 regime='fact_supersede' \u2192 edge SUPERSEDES \u2192 vers\u00e3o anterior (sem decay)\n    regime='observation_decay' \u2192 FSRS-6/stability_score normal\n  \u2192 trigger/invoke embed-mcorch-node \u2192 pgvector(768) (gr\u00e1tis)\n  \u2192 INSERT mcorch_edges (n\u00f3 \u2192 n\u00f3 do run/query \u2014 instrumenta KPI do cruzado)\n  \u2192 INSERT vision_artifacts manifest (p/ erasure cascade)\n```\n\n---\n\n## 4. API Contract Definitions\n\n> Todo request passa, nesta ordem: **identity (ES256 JWKS | PAT hash) \u2192 sentinel.inspectPrompt \u2192 d\u00e9bito mcoCoins \u2192 handler**. Bloqueio de sentinel, recusa red-line e 402 nunca debitam. Erros espelham o modelo do FRD \u00a74.4 (HTTP no transporte + `structuredContent` no tool result).\n\n### 4.1 Transport & Discovery Endpoints\n\n| Endpoint | Method | Auth | Contract |\n|----------|--------|------|----------|\n| `https://mcp.mcorch.com/` | POST/GET | Bearer (JWT\\|PAT) | MCP Streamable HTTP 2025-11-25; headers `Authorization`, `Origin` (allowlist \u2192 403 `origin_invalid`), `MCP-Protocol-Version: 2025-11-25` (mismatch \u2192 400) |\n| `/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource` | GET | p\u00fablico | RFC 9728 PRM (aponta o AS \u2014 OTD-VM-020) |\n| `/health` | GET | loopback/watchdog | `200 {status:'ok', uptime_s, tools_registered}` |\n\nUnauthenticated `initialize` (literal \u2014 gate de teste AT-VM-002):\n```\nHTTP/1.1 401 Unauthorized\nWWW-Authenticate: Bearer resource_metadata=\"https://mcp.mcorch.com/.well-known/oauth-protected-resource\"\n```\n\n### 4.2 Tool Registry (superf\u00edcie completa \u2014 classes mco do FRD/BRD; fix s\u00f3 ap\u00f3s medi\u00e7\u00e3o material, OTD-VM-004)\n\n| Tool | mco | Scope (PAT) | Gates espec\u00edficos |\n|------|-----|-------------|-------------------|\n| `mesh.search` | 0\u20131 | `mesh:read` | RLS-scoped ao tenant via `match_mcorch_nodes` |\n| `vision.describe_image` | 2 | `vision:read` | https-only + host allowlist + \u226420MB + content-type |\n| `vision.detect_objects` | 2 | `vision:read` | idem (Gemini native boxes 0\u20131000) |\n| `vision.segment` | 2 (fal) / 5 (Replicate) | `vision:read` | pista Apache default (SAM 2); SAM 3 gated FM-VM-08 |\n| `vision.ocr` | 1/p\u00e1gina | `vision:read` | provenance tag + sentinel L2 no texto |\n| `vision.analyze_video` | 2/min | `vision:read` | cap dura\u00e7\u00e3o \u226460min default res |\n| `motion.peripheral_scan` | 0\u20131 | `vision:read` | `experimental` at\u00e9 GA (recall \u2014 OTD-VM-001) |\n| `motion.track` | 1 | `vision:read` | v\u00eddeo curto \u22648s GPU; keep-warm (FM-VM-12) |\n| `deepsearch.scrape` | 1 | `deepsearch:run` | robots.txt + login-wall block + allowlist + CDSM Art.4 |\n| `deepsearch.run` | 3 | `deepsearch:run` | job async; d\u00e9bito integral na entrada |\n| `deepsearch.poll` | 0 | `deepsearch:run` | tenant-bound (`job_id` alheio \u2192 404) |\n| `mesh.consolidate_reference` | 1 | `mesh:write` | PII-strip + provenance + regime + NUNCA system node |\n| `vision.alt_text` | 2 | `vision:read` | duty-of-care gate + confidence flag (FR-VM-012/014) |\n| `vision.describe_screen` | 2 | `vision:read` | duty-of-care gate + incerteza sinalizada (FR-VM-013/014) |\n\n**Vocabul\u00e1rio de scopes (fechado):** `vision:read`, `deepsearch:run`, `mesh:read`, `mesh:write`. **NUNCA** existem scopes `credentials:*`/`billing:*` (FM-VM-06 \u2014 vazamento de PAT n\u00e3o pode alcan\u00e7ar gest\u00e3o de credencial nem carteira al\u00e9m do consumo de tools).\n\n### 4.3 Tool Contracts (completos \u2014 billing point expl\u00edcito)\n\n#### `vision.describe_image` (FR-VM-018 \u00b7 2 mco)\n\n```typescript\n// Input (sentinel-inspected)\ninterface DescribeImageInput {\n  image_url: string        // https only; host allowlist; \u226420MB; content-type imagem\n  question?: string        // QA opcional\n  assistive?: boolean      // ativa duty-of-care gate (FR-VM-014)\n  consolidate?: boolean    // grava n\u00f3 na malha (regime observation_decay)\n}\n// Output (structuredContent)\ninterface DescribeImageOutput {\n  description: string\n  confidence: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low'   // hedge obrigat\u00f3rio se 'low'\n  cost_mco: number\n  node_id?: string                         // uuid se consolidado\n}\n// mcoCoins deduction point:\n// \u2192 billing/debit.ts#debitOrThrow \u2014 AP\u00d3S identity.verify() + sentinel.inspectPrompt(),\n//   ANTES de qualquer leg de provider:\n//   POST {SUPABASE_URL}/rest/v1/rpc/deduct_mco_coins\n//     { p_user_id: <sub verificado>, p_amount: 2,\n//       p_action: 'vision.describe_image', p_context: { request_id } }\n//   (auth do POST: secret de NOME SB_SECRET_KEY \u2014 server-side only)\n// \u2192 402 insufficient_balance (nada debitado) \u00b7 BYOK \u2192 metering custo 0\n// \u2192 refundMco(2) em QUALQUER falha p\u00f3s-d\u00e9bito (idempotente por request_id; nunca lan\u00e7a)\n```\n\n#### `vision.ocr` (FR-VM-019 \u00b7 1 mco/p\u00e1gina)\n\n```typescript\ninterface OcrInput { document_url: string; format?: 'markdown' | 'json'; pages?: number }\ninterface OcrOutput { content: string; pages_processed: number; provenance: { source_url: string }; cost_mco: number }\n// Backend: Mistral OCR 3 (chave per-user mistral_api_key \u2014 integra\u00e7\u00e3o direta; N\u00c3O est\u00e1 no\n// OpenRouter) OU PaddleOCR CPU in-container (sem leg externa).\n// D\u00e9bito = pages \u00d7 1 na entrada (pages declarado); texto extra\u00eddo passa sentinel L2 antes\n// de qualquer insert (FR-VM-005b). Falha em p\u00e1gina \u2192 refund proporcional (regra \u00a74.5 FRD).\n```\n\n#### `deepsearch.run` / `deepsearch.poll` (FR-VM-007 \u00b7 3 / 0 mco)\n\n```typescript\ninterface DeepsearchRunInput  { query: string; planned_units?: number; source_allowlist?: string[] }\ninterface DeepsearchRunOutput { job_id: string; state: 'queued'; planned_units: number; charged_mco: number }\ninterface DeepsearchPollInput  { job_id: string }\ninterface DeepsearchPollOutput {\n  job_id: string\n  state: 'queued' | 'running' | 'done' | 'partial' | 'failed'\n  delivered_units: number; planned_units: number\n  partial: boolean; failed_units?: string[]; refunded_mco?: number\n  result?: { references: Array<{ title: string; url: string; license?: string; summary: string; node_id?: string }> }\n}\n// D\u00e9bito integral (3) na entrada \u2014 402 \u2192 job n\u00e3o nasce. planned_units \u2264 cap do plano (DD-VM-003).\n// Partial-delivery refund EXATAMENTE como selado no FRD \u00a74.5: retida = max(1, floor(3\u00d7delivered/planned)),\n// refund = 3\u2212retida via refundMco ANTES do estado terminal, floor sempre A FAVOR do tenant,\n// refund no m\u00e1ximo 1\u00d7 por job_id (guard em vision_jobs.refunded_at). Poll de outro tenant \u2192 404.\n```\n\n#### `mesh.consolidate_reference` (FR-VM-021/015 \u00b7 1 mco)\n\n```typescript\ninterface ConsolidateInput {\n  content: Record<string, unknown>\n  regime: 'observation_decay' | 'fact_supersede'           // obrigat\u00f3rio (FR-VM-015)\n  provenance: { source_url: string; license: string; origin: 'tenant' | 'scraped' }\n  retention_until: string                                   // ISO; > now(); \u2264 teto (DD-VM-004)\n  supersedes_node_id?: string                               // exigido se fact_supersede\n}\ninterface ConsolidateOutput { node_id: string; embedded: boolean; cost_mco: number }\n// D\u00e9bito 1 na entrada. PII-strip + sentinel L2 ANTES do insert; origin='scraped' exige\n// source_url+license e NUNCA system node (user_id NOT NULL \u2014 guard server-side, viola\u00e7\u00e3o = bug cr\u00edtico).\n// Embed-on-insert via embed-mcorch-node (gr\u00e1tis). Edge ao n\u00f3 do run sela a traceabilidade.\n```\n\n#### `vision.alt_text` (FR-VM-012/014 \u00b7 2 mco \u2014 assistiva)\n\n```typescript\ninterface AltTextInput  { image_url: string; context?: string; lang?: 'pt-BR' | 'en' }\ninterface AltTextOutput {\n  alt_short: string; alt_long: string; decorative: boolean\n  confidence: 'high' | 'medium' | 'low'\n  human_review_required: boolean          // true sempre que confidence != 'high'\n  escalation_hint?: string                // gancho de verifica\u00e7\u00e3o humana (padr\u00e3o Aira)\n}\n// Duty-of-care gate ANTES do d\u00e9bito: query red-line (medica\u00e7\u00e3o/dosagem/al\u00e9rgeno/mobilidade/\n// emerg\u00eancia \u2014 template ToS Be My Eyes) \u2192 403 assistive_redline_refusal, SEM descri\u00e7\u00e3o gerada,\n// SEM cobran\u00e7a, logado em infra_health_logs. Claim \"WCAG 2.2 SC 1.1.1 / NBR 17225-conformante\"\n// \u00e9 PROIBIDO sem caminho de revis\u00e3o humana + mapeamento OTD-VM-019 (FM-VM-17).\n```\n\n#### Contratos condensados (mesmo envelope identity\u2192sentinel\u2192d\u00e9bito\u2192refund)\n\n| Tool | Input (ess\u00eancia) | Output (ess\u00eancia) |\n|------|------------------|-------------------|\n| `vision.detect_objects` | `{ image_url, classes?: string[] }` | `{ objects: [{label, box_2d:[ymin,xmin,ymax,xmax], confidence}], cost_mco }` (boxes normalizados 0\u20131000 \u2014 Gemini) |\n| `vision.segment` | `{ image_url, prompt: { text? \\| box? \\| point? } }` | `{ masks: [{rle, label?}], backend: 'sam2'\\|'sam3', cost_mco }` |\n| `vision.analyze_video` | `{ video_url, question, max_minutes? }` | `{ answer, timeline?: [{t, event}], minutes_billed, cost_mco }` |\n| `motion.peripheral_scan` | `{ source_url, mode: 'image'\\|'frames', sensitivity? }` | `{ salient_regions: [{box, score}], resource_links: string[], triggered: boolean, cost_mco }` |\n| `motion.track` | `{ video_url (\u22648s), targets?: 'pose'\\|'objects' }` | `{ tracks: [{id, keypoints?\\|boxes, frames}], cost_mco }` |\n| `deepsearch.scrape` | `{ url, format?: 'markdown' }` | `{ markdown, provenance: {source_url, license?, robots_ok: true}, cost_mco }` |\n| `mesh.search` | `{ query, top_k?, node_types?: string[] }` | `{ matches: [{node_id, similarity, snippet, node_type}], cost_mco }` |\n| `vision.describe_screen` | `{ screenshot_url, focus?: string }` | `{ description, elements?: [{role, label}], confidence, human_review_required }` |\n\n### 4.4 Resources & Prompts (FR-VM-025 \u2014 progressive enhancement; toda capability externa mant\u00e9m porta tool)\n\n| Surface | URI / name | Notes |\n|---------|-----------|-------|\n| Resource | `mesh://nodes/{node_id}` | RLS-scoped ao tenant |\n| Resource | `mesh://references?pillar={cv\\|motion\\|neuromorphic\\|foveated}` | Corpus consolidado por pilar |\n| Resource | `vision://artifacts/{content_ref}` | Storage privado `vision-artifacts`, signed URL curto |\n| Prompt | `/vision-essence` \u00b7 `/reference-brief` \u00b7 `/competitive-vision` | Workflows empacotados (espelham as skills PT-BR do Plugin Zip) |\n\n### 4.5 Error Model\n\nId\u00eantico ao FRD \u00a74.4 (fonte selada \u2014 n\u00e3o duplicado aqui linha a linha): `400 protocol_version_mismatch` \u00b7 `401 identity_unverified` \u00b7 `402 insufficient_balance` / `<service>_not_configured` \u00b7 `403 origin_invalid` / `sentinel_blocked` / `scope_insufficient` / `assistive_redline_refusal` \u00b7 `404 job_not_found` \u00b7 `422 validation_failed` (mensagem PT-BR) \u00b7 `429 rate_limited` \u00b7 `500 internal_error` (refund se j\u00e1 debitado). Implementa\u00e7\u00e3o: m\u00f3dulo \u00fanico `errors.ts` mapeia identifier \u2192 HTTP + `structuredContent` espelhado.\n\n---\n\n## 5. Security Design\n\n### 5.1 Threat Model \u2014 STRIDE per component (absorve FM-VM-01..18 verbatim)\n\n| Component | FM ID | Threat | STRIDE | Severity | Mitigation (design gate) |\n|-----------|-------|--------|--------|----------|--------------------------|\n| Transport/Identity (`auth/jwks.ts`) | FM-VM-01 | 1\u00ba servi\u00e7o tenant-facing fora do gateway Supabase; bug fail-open = impersona\u00e7\u00e3o cross-tenant | Spoofing / Elevation | **Cr\u00edtica** | JWKS ES256 verify ANTES de confiar no `sub`; fail-closed se JWKS inalcan\u00e7\u00e1vel; `SB_SECRET_KEY` jamais em path client-reachable; Origin allowlist 403 |\n| Tool args (todas) | FM-VM-02 | Prompt-injection inbound nos args manipula o agente | Tampering | Alta | `sentinel.inspectPrompt` L1/L2/L3 p\u00f3s-identidade, pr\u00e9-d\u00e9bito; bloqueio\u2192403, 0 mco, logado |\n| Mesh writer | FM-VM-03 | Conte\u00fado web hostil scrapeado vira n\u00f3 persistente consumido pelo Pantheon (inje\u00e7\u00e3o persistente) | Tampering | Alta | Sentinel L2 sobre texto EXTRA\u00cdDO pr\u00e9-insert; tag `origin:'scraped'` p/ trust por proveni\u00eancia; bloqueado nunca vira n\u00f3 |\n| OCR/VLM outputs | FM-VM-04 | Instru\u00e7\u00f5es renderizadas DENTRO da imagem lavadas via OCR/VLM para o loop do agente | Tampering | M\u00e9dia-Alta | Sentinel L2 estendido a TODO output de vision-tool (OWASP LLM01 / MCP tool-poisoning); origem `vision_extracted` no metadata |\n| Credentials (`auth/credentials.ts`) | FM-VM-05 | `FIRECRAWL_API_KEY` compartilhada em fluxo user-facing \u2192 exaust\u00e3o de quota + blast radius financeiro cross-tenant | DoS / Repudiation | Alta | Coluna `firecrawl_api_key` per-user + UI + 402 `firecrawl_not_configured` fail-closed; zero env-key global em path user-facing (grep material no gate de teste) |\n| PAT (`mcp_access_tokens`) | FM-VM-06 | Vazamento de PAT = dreno direto da carteira mcoCoins | Spoofing | **Cr\u00edtica** | Hash SHA-256 only (nunca plaintext/Vault-revers\u00edvel); scopes fechados SEM `credentials:*`/`billing:*`; `expires_at`/`revoked_at`; RLS default-deny; `/security-review` na migration |\n| Jobs engine | FM-VM-07 | Job parcial (5 de 12 p\u00e1ginas) com sem\u00e2ntica de refund amb\u00edgua | Repudiation | M\u00e9dia | Regra selada FRD \u00a74.5: floor a favor do tenant, refund idempotente 1\u00d7/job_id, `partial:true` + `refunded_mco` transparentes |\n| Model matrix (\u00a72.2) | FM-VM-08 | Ultralytics AGPL servido em rede sem Enterprise = viola\u00e7\u00e3o; SAM 3/DINOv3 propagam termos | Compliance | Alta | Pista Apache default; AGPL banido; gated models s\u00f3 com LICENSE fetchado + propaga\u00e7\u00e3o documentada (OTD-VM-006) |\n| Vision tools (escopo) | FM-VM-09 | Faces/gait = dado biom\u00e9trico sens\u00edvel (LGPD art. 5\u00ba II); leg\u00edtimo interesse N\u00c3O se aplica (art. 11) | Information Disclosure / Compliance | **Cr\u00edtica** | Escopo HARD: detec\u00e7\u00e3o/descri\u00e7\u00e3o APENAS \u2014 zero gera\u00e7\u00e3o de template, zero recognition, zero classifica\u00e7\u00e3o biom\u00e9trica (\u00a75.5); watch ANPD 2026 (OTD-VM-007/008) |\n| Mesh writer + providers | FM-VM-10 | Screenshots/scraping capturam PII de terceiros; envio a APIs US = transfer\u00eancia internacional sem adequa\u00e7\u00e3o | Information Disclosure | Alta | PII-strip server-side pr\u00e9-insert; SCCs ANPD (Res. 19/2024) no DPA do provider OU roteamento adequado OU consentimento art. 33 VIII (auditoria OTD-VM-005 antes de provider default) |\n| Erasure path | FM-VM-11 | N\u00f3s persistentes/embeddados/bridgeados sem path de apagamento (precedente hiQ alcan\u00e7ou corpus derivado) | Compliance | Alta | RPC `erase_vision_artifacts` cascade (n\u00f3+edges+embedding+Storage+registro provider via manifest `vision_artifacts`); retention clock art. 16 + sweep di\u00e1rio; allowlist de fontes permissivas; robots.txt + CDSM Art. 4 |\n| GPU legs (`motion.track`) | FM-VM-12 | Cold start 10\u201360s estoura o budget de 1 mco | DoS (econ\u00f4mico) | M\u00e9dia | Keep-warm ou batch semantics; medi\u00e7\u00e3o material do $ antes do fix de classe (OTD-VM-004) |\n| Processo | FM-VM-13 | C\u00f3digo sem BoK suite viola Closed-Loop Protocol | Process | Bloqueante | Esta su\u00edte (01\u201305) sela o gate; SOPs `docs/processes/vision-mcp-*.md` antes de automa\u00e7\u00e3o (Lei 2) |\n| Assistive gate | FM-VM-14 | Descri\u00e7\u00e3o errada p/ usu\u00e1rio BLV dependente = dano f\u00edsico real; overtrust em IA confiante (ASSETS 2024) | Safety | **Cr\u00edtica** | Recusas categ\u00f3ricas red-line 100% (zero-cost por design \u2014 recusa n\u00e3o chama provider); confidence/hedge; escalonamento humano; recusa logada e n\u00e3o cobrada |\n| Branding/superf\u00edcie | FM-VM-15 | \"Cognitive computing\"/\"c\u00e9rebro digital\"/consci\u00eancia \u2192 agent-washing + risco reputacional | Repudiation | M\u00e9dia | \u00c2ncoras peer-reviewed only (CoALA/V*/GraphRAG); r\u00f3tulos proibidos em c\u00f3digo, docs e marketing (lint de copy no release checklist) |\n| Mesh persistence | FM-VM-16 | Decay FSRS aplicado a fatos sem\u00e2nticos corrompe a malha (category error arXiv 2604.11364) | Tampering (integridade) | M\u00e9dia | Regime obrigat\u00f3rio no insert (FR-VM-015): `observation_decay` vs `fact_supersede` + edge SUPERSEDES |\n| Assistive claims | FM-VM-17 | Claim \"NBR 17225/WCAG-conformante\" sem servir \"prop\u00f3sito equivalente\" \u2192 exposi\u00e7\u00e3o EAA/LBI Art. 63 | Repudiation / Compliance | Alta | `human_review_required` no contrato; relat\u00f3rio de conformidade sela s\u00f3 itens revisados (FR-VM-026); mapeamento requisito-a-requisito pendente (OTD-VM-019) |\n| Escopo produto | FM-VM-18 | Overreach de l\u00edngua de sinais (avatar/MT) cruza red line WFD+WASLI | Compliance | M\u00e9dia | LIBRAS/sign language FORA do v1 (hard exclusion no registry de tools); reentrada s\u00f3 com scope experimental + revis\u00e3o da comunidade Surda (OTD-VM-018) |\n\n### 5.2 RLS Policy Definitions\n\n```sql\n-- mcp_access_tokens: default-deny; tenant-only; DELETE n\u00e3o exposto (revoga\u00e7\u00e3o = UPDATE revoked_at)\nALTER TABLE public.mcp_access_tokens ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n\nCREATE POLICY \"mcp_tokens_select_own\"\n  ON public.mcp_access_tokens FOR SELECT\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"mcp_tokens_insert_own\"\n  ON public.mcp_access_tokens FOR INSERT\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n\nCREATE POLICY \"mcp_tokens_update_own\"   -- s\u00f3 para revoga\u00e7\u00e3o (revoked_at)\n  ON public.mcp_access_tokens FOR UPDATE\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid())\n  WITH CHECK (user_id = auth.uid());\n-- Lookup do container roda via service-role (bypassa RLS) \u2014 o container \u00e9 o \u00daNICO leitor do hash.\n\n-- vision_jobs / vision_artifacts: SELECT pr\u00f3prio (usage/poll via dashboard); writes s\u00f3 service-role\nALTER TABLE public.vision_jobs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"vision_jobs_select_own\"\n  ON public.vision_jobs FOR SELECT\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n-- (sem INSERT/UPDATE/DELETE policies para authenticated \u2014 default-deny; o container escreve via service key)\n\nALTER TABLE public.vision_artifacts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\nCREATE POLICY \"vision_artifacts_select_own\"\n  ON public.vision_artifacts FOR SELECT\n  USING (user_id = auth.uid());\n-- DELETE s\u00f3 via erase_vision_artifacts (SECURITY DEFINER auth.uid()-scoped) ou retention sweep (service-role).\n```\n\n### 5.3 Identity Validation Points (ES256 JWKS + PAT \u2014 NUNCA `atob`)\n\nSOP de refer\u00eancia: `docs/processes/edge-jwt-identity-verification.md` (gates G1/G2 em `scripts/qa/test-es256-jwt-verification.ts`).\n\n```typescript\n// auth/jwks.ts \u2014 caminho JWT (Supabase user session)\nimport { createRemoteJWKSet, jwtVerify } from 'jose'\nconst JWKS = createRemoteJWKSet(new URL(`${SUPABASE_URL}/auth/v1/.well-known/jwks.json`))\n// fail-closed: QUALQUER erro (rede, kid desconhecido, alg != ES256, exp) \u2192 401, nunca processa\nconst { payload } = await jwtVerify(token, JWKS, { algorithms: ['ES256'] })\nconst userId = payload.sub  // \u00daNICA fonte de identidade para deduct_mco_coins (anti-confused-deputy)\n\n// auth/pat.ts \u2014 caminho PAT\nconst hash = sha256hex(token)                       // lookup O(1) por token_hash\n// service-role SELECT: revoked_at IS NULL AND expires_at > now() \u2014 sen\u00e3o 401\n```\n\n**Anti-patterns proibidos (herdados do SOP):** `atob(token.split('.')[1])` cego para extrair `sub`; confiar em `user_id` vindo de body/header n\u00e3o-verificado; processar request com JWKS inalcan\u00e7\u00e1vel (\"fail-open\"); aceitar bearer em query string; token passthrough para providers downstream (proibi\u00e7\u00e3o expl\u00edcita da spec MCP 2025-11-25).\n\n### 5.4 Secrets Inventory (NOMES apenas \u2014 valores jamais em doc/c\u00f3digo/commit)\n\n| Secret Name | Used In | Location | Scope rule |\n|-------------|---------|----------|------------|\n| `SB_SECRET_KEY` | container (PostgREST/RPC: deduct/refund/mesh/jobs/logs) | env do container (compose, sem valor no repo) | **Server-side only**; jamais em path client-reachable; legadas `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`/`ANON_KEY` REVOGADAS |\n| `OPENROUTER_API_KEY` | fallback de embed/system flows | Supabase Vault | **Fallback-restricted**: S\u00d3 cron/service-role/system; nunca caminho prim\u00e1rio user-facing (API Tenancy) |\n| `user_api_keys.firecrawl_api_key` (per-user) | `deepsearch.*` | Vault via `decrypted_user_api_keys` (service-role only view) | Prim\u00e1rio; ausente \u2192 402 `firecrawl_not_configured` |\n| `user_api_keys.openrouter_api_key` / `google_api_key` / `openai_api_key` (per-user) | `vision.*` VLM legs | idem | Prim\u00e1rio (BYOK \u2192 metering custo 0) |\n| `user_api_keys.mistral_api_key` (per-user, nova) | `vision.ocr` (Mistral OCR 3 direto) | idem | Prim\u00e1rio; ausente \u2192 402 `mistral_not_configured` (PaddleOCR CPU \u00e9 o caminho sem chave) |\n| PAT do tenant (`mcp_access_tokens.token_hash`) | clients MCP externos | DB (hash SHA-256 apenas) | Plaintext exibido 1\u00d7 no client; nunca armazenado |\n\n### 5.5 LGPD Design (profile ELEVADO)\n\n1. **Exclus\u00e3o de escopo biom\u00e9trico (FM-VM-09 \u2014 hard rule de produto):** as tools fazem **detec\u00e7\u00e3o e descri\u00e7\u00e3o** apenas. \u00c9 PROIBIDO no design: gera\u00e7\u00e3o/armazenamento de template biom\u00e9trico, reconhecimento/matching de face, identifica\u00e7\u00e3o por gait, classifica\u00e7\u00e3o biom\u00e9trica de pessoas. N\u00e3o existe tool de recognition no registry (\u00a74.2) e o gate de teste NFR-VM-009 verifica \"zero tool de recognition publicada\". Base legal art. 11 nunca \u00e9 assumida \u2014 qualquer evolu\u00e7\u00e3o nesse sentido exige nova rodada BoK + watch ANPD (OTD-VM-007/008).\n2. **PII-strip pr\u00e9-mesh (FM-VM-10):** m\u00f3dulo `mesh/pii-strip.ts` remove PII de terceiros (nomes/e-mails/telefones/documentos detectados) ANTES de qualquer insert em `mcorch_nodes`; conte\u00fado scraped sempre `user_id NOT NULL` (nunca system node \u2014 soberania BR-VM-005).\n3. **Erasure em cascata (FR-VM-009, FM-VM-11):** `erase_vision_artifacts(content_ref)` SECURITY DEFINER, `search_path=''`, EXECUTE revogado de `anon`, guard `auth.uid()` = dono do artefato (regra house: todo SECURITY DEFINER novo \u00e9 edge-only OU auth.uid()-guarded). Cascade: n\u00f3s + edges + embeddings + objetos do bucket `vision-artifacts` + registro de c\u00f3pias provider (manifest `vision_artifacts.provider_copies`). Prova material: `SELECT` count antes/depois = zero res\u00edduo \u226424h (NFR-VM-009).\n4. **Retention clock (art. 16):** todo n\u00f3/artefato de vis\u00e3o carrega `retention_until` (default e teto em DD-VM-004); sweep di\u00e1rio in-container expira vencidos pelo mesmo cascade.\n5. **Transfer\u00eancia internacional:** envio de imagem/texto a providers US = transfer\u00eancia internacional **sem adequa\u00e7\u00e3o** (s\u00f3 UE via Res. 32/2026). Mitiga\u00e7\u00e3o de design: BYOK per-user (o tenant escolhe e contrata o provider) + DPA com SCCs ANPD (Res. 19/2024) auditado antes de qualquer provider default do sistema (OTD-VM-005); documenta\u00e7\u00e3o do fluxo em `/dashboard/settings`.\n6. **Duty-of-care assistivo (FM-VM-14/17):** red-lines categ\u00f3ricas, confidence/hedge, escalonamento humano e `human_review_required` \u2014 ver \u00a74.3 e FR-VM-014/026.\n\n---\n\n## 6. Infrastructure Touchpoints\n\n| Infrastructure | Usage | Impact |\n|----------------|-------|--------|\n| Docker Sovereign Mesh (`mcorch-sovereign-net`) | Novo servi\u00e7o `mcorch_vision_mcp` (anchor `x-hardening`, UID 1001, `read_only`, loopback `127.0.0.1:3200`) | +1 container; sem `name:` no compose (caveat material) |\n| nginx (host) + Cloudflare | Novo vhost `mcp.mcorch.com` \u2192 proxy loopback; `proxy_buffering off`; SSE heartbeat \u226425s anti idle ~100s | Exige root p/ sites-enabled + DNS CF (OTD-VM-013) |\n| `scripts/watchdog-mcorch.sh` (cron */5) | Aprende o container novo \u2192 telemetria `infra_health_logs` | Neural HUD via `get-infra-status` sem novo encanamento |\n| `infra_health_logs` | Novos event types `service='vision-mcp'`: `tool_error`, `provider_degraded`, `job_partial`, `job_failed`, `assistive_refusal`, `jwks_unreachable`, `retention_sweep`, `container_health`; `service='sentinel'`: `sentinel_block` | Watchdog telemetry (NFR-VM-010 \u2014 100% dos paths) |\n| `mcorch_nodes`/`mcorch_edges` + pgvector | **O store de mem\u00f3ria \u00e9 o mesh pgvector \u2014 ChromaDB N\u00c3O entra no path** (`mcorch_chroma` permanece fora deste m\u00f3dulo) | Mesh growth; embed gr\u00e1tis via `embed-mcorch-node` |\n| `deduct_mco_coins` RPC + `agent_executions` | Billing at\u00f4mico + metering por leg (reuso integral) | Zero encanamento novo de billing |\n| Supabase Storage | Bucket privado `vision-artifacts` (frames/crops; signed URL curto) | Erasure cascade alcan\u00e7a o bucket |\n| MCP Registry (preview) | `server.json` via `mcp-publisher`, namespace verificado `com.mcorch/*` (challenge DNS/HTTP) | Descoberta externa (FR-VM-011) |\n\n**MCP version-pinning & deprecation-watch (NFR-VM-013):** pin na revis\u00e3o **2025-11-25** (\"Current\"); `MCP-Protocol-Version` negociado por request; checklist de release inclui: (a) diff contra a spec corrente, (b) status do RC 2026-07-28, (c) status do registry (preview \u2014 breaking changes esperadas), (d) lint de copy anti-FM-VM-15. SDK `@modelcontextprotocol/sdk` pinned por minor.\n\n---\n\n## 7. Migration Stubs\n\n> Ordem de aplica\u00e7\u00e3o = ordem abaixo. **Cada migration passa `/security-review` antes do commit** (mandato FMEA-011 + NFR-VM-007). Padr\u00f5es herdados: RLS default-deny, `search_path=''` em SECURITY DEFINER, EXECUTE revogado por default.\n\n```sql\n-- Migration 1: <ts>_vision_mcp_access_tokens.sql  (FR-VM-003 \u00b7 FM-VM-06)\nCREATE TABLE public.mcp_access_tokens (\n  id            uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  user_id       uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  token_hash    char(64) NOT NULL UNIQUE,        -- SHA-256 hex; NUNCA plaintext/Vault-revers\u00edvel\n  token_prefix  text NOT NULL,                   -- ex.: 'mcorch_pat_3f9a\u2026' p/ exibi\u00e7\u00e3o\n  label         text,\n  scopes        text[] NOT NULL,                 -- \u2286 {vision:read, deepsearch:run, mesh:read, mesh:write}\n  expires_at    timestamptz NOT NULL,            -- \u2264 now() + interval '365 days' (DD-VM-002; CHECK na app+DB)\n  revoked_at    timestamptz,\n  last_used_at  timestamptz,\n  created_at    timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  CONSTRAINT scopes_no_privileged CHECK (\n    NOT (scopes && ARRAY['credentials:read','credentials:write','billing:read','billing:write'])\n  )\n);\nALTER TABLE public.mcp_access_tokens ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n-- (policies de \u00a75.2 \u2014 default-deny; sem DELETE; revoga\u00e7\u00e3o = UPDATE revoked_at)\nCREATE INDEX mcp_tokens_hash_idx ON public.mcp_access_tokens (token_hash) WHERE revoked_at IS NULL;\nCREATE INDEX mcp_tokens_user_idx ON public.mcp_access_tokens (user_id);\n```\n\n```sql\n-- Migration 2: <ts>_user_api_keys_vision_byok.sql  (FR-VM-004 \u00b7 FM-VM-05)\n-- Segue VERBATIM o precedente aditivo 20260601060000_user_api_keys_apify_token.sql:\n--   base column \u2192 bloco no encrypt trigger \u2192 coluna mascarada na VIEW p\u00fablica \u2192\n--   INSTEAD OF trigger (cols/VALUES + COALESCE/NULLIF do mask) \u2192 decrypted view (vault join).\nALTER TABLE public.user_api_keys_table\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS firecrawl_api_key text,\n  ADD COLUMN IF NOT EXISTS mistral_api_key  text;\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.user_api_keys_table.firecrawl_api_key\n  IS 'Per-user Firecrawl key (BYOK) for vision-mcp deepsearch. Vault-encrypted. Ausente = 402 firecrawl_not_configured (fail-closed \u2014 sem fallback global user-facing).';\nCOMMENT ON COLUMN public.user_api_keys_table.mistral_api_key\n  IS 'Per-user Mistral key (BYOK) for vision.ocr (Mistral OCR 3 direct \u2014 not on OpenRouter). Vault-encrypted.';\n-- (+ re-create de trg_encrypt_user_api_keys / user_api_keys VIEW / trg_instead_of_user_api_keys /\n--    decrypted_user_api_keys com os DOIS campos apensados \u2014 todos os handlers existentes preservados verbatim)\n```\n\n```sql\n-- Migration 3: <ts>_vision_mcp_jobs.sql  (FR-VM-007 \u00b7 FM-VM-07)\nCREATE TABLE public.vision_jobs (\n  id              uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,   -- = job_id do contrato\n  user_id         uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  project_id      uuid,\n  tool            text NOT NULL,                                -- 'deepsearch.run'\n  state           text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'queued'\n                    CHECK (state IN ('queued','running','done','partial','failed')),\n  planned_units   int NOT NULL DEFAULT 1 CHECK (planned_units >= 1),  -- cap por plano: app-side (DD-VM-003)\n  delivered_units int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,\n  charged_mco     int NOT NULL,\n  refunded_mco    int,\n  refunded_at     timestamptz,                                  -- guard de idempot\u00eancia (refund 1\u00d7/job)\n  failed_units    jsonb,\n  result          jsonb,\n  expires_at      timestamptz NOT NULL,                         -- terminal + 24h (reten\u00e7\u00e3o de poll)\n  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),\n  updated_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\nALTER TABLE public.vision_jobs ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n-- (SELECT own apenas \u2014 \u00a75.2; writes via service key do container)\nCREATE INDEX vision_jobs_user_state_idx ON public.vision_jobs (user_id, state);\n```\n\n```sql\n-- Migration 4: <ts>_vision_mcp_artifacts.sql  (FR-VM-009 \u00b7 FM-VM-11)\nCREATE TABLE public.vision_artifacts (\n  id              uuid DEFAULT gen_random_uuid() PRIMARY KEY,\n  content_ref     text NOT NULL UNIQUE,                 -- chave do cascade de erasure\n  user_id         uuid REFERENCES auth.users(id) ON DELETE CASCADE NOT NULL,\n  project_id      uuid,\n  node_ids        uuid[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',         -- n\u00f3s mcorch_nodes derivados\n  storage_paths   text[] NOT NULL DEFAULT '{}',         -- objetos no bucket vision-artifacts\n  provider_copies jsonb NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]'::jsonb,   -- registro de c\u00f3pias enviadas a providers (FM-VM-11)\n  retention_until timestamptz NOT NULL,                 -- > now(); \u2264 teto DD-VM-004 (sweep di\u00e1rio)\n  created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()\n);\nALTER TABLE public.vision_artifacts ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;\n-- (SELECT own apenas \u2014 \u00a75.2)\nCREATE INDEX vision_artifacts_retention_idx ON public.vision_artifacts (retention_until);\nCREATE INDEX vision_artifacts_user_idx ON public.vision_artifacts (user_id);\n```\n\n```sql\n-- Migration 5: <ts>_vision_mcp_erase_rpc.sql  (FR-VM-009 \u2014 espelha erase_lead())\nCREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.erase_vision_artifacts(p_content_ref text)\nRETURNS jsonb\nLANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = ''\nAS $$\nDECLARE v_artifact public.vision_artifacts%ROWTYPE;\nBEGIN\n  SELECT * INTO v_artifact FROM public.vision_artifacts\n   WHERE content_ref = p_content_ref\n     AND (user_id = auth.uid() OR auth.role() = 'service_role');  -- auth.uid()-guard (regra ledger-RPC)\n  IF NOT FOUND THEN RAISE EXCEPTION 'artifact_not_found'; END IF;\n  DELETE FROM public.mcorch_edges  WHERE source_id = ANY(v_artifact.node_ids) OR target_id = ANY(v_artifact.node_ids);\n  DELETE FROM public.mcorch_nodes  WHERE id = ANY(v_artifact.node_ids);          -- leva o embedding junto\n  DELETE FROM storage.objects      WHERE bucket_id = 'vision-artifacts' AND name = ANY(v_artifact.storage_paths);\n  DELETE FROM public.vision_artifacts WHERE id = v_artifact.id;\n  RETURN jsonb_build_object('erased', true, 'nodes', array_length(v_artifact.node_ids,1),\n                            'storage_objects', array_length(v_artifact.storage_paths,1),\n                            'provider_copies_registered', v_artifact.provider_copies);\nEND $$;\nREVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.erase_vision_artifacts(text) FROM PUBLIC, anon;\nGRANT  EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.erase_vision_artifacts(text) TO authenticated, service_role;\n```\n\n---\n\n## 8. Open Technical Decisions\n\n### 8.1 Decisions sealed by this SDD (deferidas pelo FRD para c\u00e1)\n\n| ID | Decision | Sealed value | Rationale |\n|----|----------|--------------|-----------|\n| DD-VM-001 | Path do pacote + porta do servi\u00e7o | `packages/vision-mcp-core/` \u00b7 `127.0.0.1:3200` | Precedente `packages/gitnexus-core/` (:3100); loopback-only |\n| DD-VM-002 | Validade do PAT | Default 90 dias; m\u00e1ximo **365 dias** (CHECK + valida\u00e7\u00e3o no hook) | Limita janela de dreno FM-VM-06 sem matar configs long-lived |\n| DD-VM-003 | Cap de `planned_units` por plano | Starter 10 \u00b7 Pro 25 \u00b7 Enterprise 50 (provis\u00f3rio \u2014 recalibra com OTD-VM-004) | Proporcional aos saldos 500/2000/10000 mco; enforced app-side no aceite do job |\n| DD-VM-004 | Retention clock (art. 16) | `retention_until` default **90 dias**; teto **365 dias**; sweep di\u00e1rio | Conservador at\u00e9 regula\u00e7\u00e3o ANPD 2026 (OTD-VM-007); tenant pode encurtar, nunca exceder o teto |\n| DD-VM-005 | Pin OpenCV | **4.x** (Apache-2.0 verificada); 5.0 s\u00f3 ap\u00f3s re-verifica\u00e7\u00e3o de licen\u00e7a + bench ARM64 | Resolve a metade decid\u00edvel de OTD-VM-010 |\n| DD-VM-006 | Acesso externo v1 | **PAT-first** (bearer \u2014 atende Claude API connector e hosts completos); OAuth 2.1 flow completo entra com OTD-VM-020 | PRM RFC 9728 j\u00e1 publicado desde o dia 1 (401 aponta o caminho); reduz o blast radius da Fatia 3 |\n\n### 8.2 OTD Register (absorve OTD-VM-001..019 verbatim do blueprint \u00a78 + OTD-VM-020 surfaced pelo FRD)\n\n| ID | Description (verbatim-condensada do blueprint) | Option A | Option B | Recommendation | Owner |\n|----|-----------------------------------------------|----------|----------|----------------|-------|\n| OTD-VM-001 | Valida\u00e7\u00e3o de dom\u00ednio da sali\u00eancia (Tier 1): DeepGaze treinado em free-viewing de imagens naturais, sem evid\u00eancia de transfer\u00eancia p/ screenshots/UIs; falta alvo de recall + custo de falso-negativo. Maior buraco t\u00e9cnico do diferencial | Probe com UEyes/V*Bench antes do GA do gate | Lan\u00e7ar gate s\u00f3 com frame-diff/MOG2 (sem sali\u00eancia ML) e medir | **B agora, A antes do GA** \u2014 `experimental` at\u00e9 recall \u22650.95 medido (FR-VM-010) | Engineering |\n| OTD-VM-002 | Demanda do produto cruzado vision+deepsearch = whitespace SEM valida\u00e7\u00e3o (emendada: assistivo \u00e9 beachhead demand-provado; cruzado segue hip\u00f3tese) | GTM ancorado no assistivo, cruzado como upsell | GTM no cruzado direto | **A** (conforme MRD bifurcado) | Sovereign |\n| OTD-VM-003 | Long-running jobs sobre MCP p/ clients externos: `tasks` experimental; Claude API connector consome s\u00f3 tools; zero exemplos de produ\u00e7\u00e3o coletados | submit\u2192job_id + poll tool (tools-only universal) | MCP tasks/elicitation quando estabilizar | **A** (j\u00e1 selado em FR-VM-007); montar matriz de capabilities por host na Fatia 3 | Engineering |\n| OTD-VM-004 | Unit economics medido: calibra\u00e7\u00e3o \u00a76 do blueprint \u00e9 inicial; falta custo GPU por-tool, tokeniza\u00e7\u00e3o exata de imagem, throughput | Medir $ real por tool em staging antes do fix | Fixar classes pela estimativa | **A** (mandato SOP `mcoin-cost-calibration.md`; NFR-VM-011) | Engineering |\n| OTD-VM-005 | DPAs/SCCs dos providers p/ inputs de imagem (zero-retention/no-training; prontid\u00e3o SCC ANPD) n\u00e3o pesquisados | Auditar DPAs antes de provider default | BYOK-only sem default do sistema | **B no v1, A antes de qualquer default** (\u00a75.5.5) | Sovereign + Engineering |\n| OTD-VM-006 | Licen\u00e7as n\u00e3o-fetchadas: texto da SAM License (field-of-use comercial), gates HF sam3/dinov3, ToS de leaderboards | Fetch dos LICENSE antes de incluir gated models | Excluir gated models do v1 | **B no v1** (pista Apache default \u00a72.2); A quando SAM 3 for desejado | Engineering |\n| OTD-VM-007 | Regula\u00e7\u00e3o biom\u00e9trica ANPD 2026 em aberto (subs\u00eddios fecharam 02/07/2025; regra final n\u00e3o publicada) | Watch item trimestral + design conservador | Aguardar regra p/ desenhar | **A** \u2014 escopo j\u00e1 \u00e9 detection/description-only (\u00a75.5.1) | Sovereign |\n| OTD-VM-008 | Pose/gait sem identifica\u00e7\u00e3o: sem orienta\u00e7\u00e3o ANPD se an\u00e1lise n\u00e3o-identificadora escapa do art. 11 | Tratar pose/gait como sens\u00edvel (gates m\u00e1ximos) | Tratar como n\u00e3o-sens\u00edvel se n\u00e3o-identificador | **A at\u00e9 a regula\u00e7\u00e3o 2026** \u2014 `motion.track` sem persist\u00eancia de s\u00e9ries por pessoa | Sovereign |\n| OTD-VM-009 | ToS Notion/Medium: cl\u00e1usulas de scraping n\u00e3o-fetch\u00e1veis/n\u00e3o confirmadas no texto vivo | Pinar wording na implementa\u00e7\u00e3o do allowlist | Bloquear esses dom\u00ednios por default | **B at\u00e9 A acontecer** (allowlist de fontes permissivas j\u00e1 \u00e9 o default \u2014 FR-VM-020) | Engineering |\n| OTD-VM-010 | Pin OpenCV 4.x-vs-5.0 + benchmarks aarch64 ausentes (n\u00fameros CPU coletados s\u00e3o x86;
